Furthermore, much of the gap persists even when models write and run their own vision code: such code is unreliable on realistic imagery, and catching its failures itself requires the active perception the models lack. Together, these results indicate that current MLLMs lack robust active visual observation, motivating architectures and training objectives that close the perception-reasoning loop.

</details>


### [99] [Vision-Language Assistant for Emotional Reactions to Risky Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16181)
*Harine Choi,Eun Hak Lee,Zhengzhong Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Keep Yelling Assistant (KYA)系统，一个结合视觉感知与情感化语言生成的驾驶辅助框架。视觉模块使用YOLOv8变体检测危险驾驶行为（如突然切入），提取并标准化距离、速度、到达时间等关键指标；语言模块基于用户偏好（中性、幽默、分析等情感风格），利用ChatGPT-4o、Claude 3等大语言模型生成情感化回应。用户研究（108人）显示YOLOv8s+ChatGPT-4o组合评分最高（4.29/5.00），所有模型均获得积极评价，但偏好因人格类型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型在自动驾驶中虽具备感知与推理能力，但很少考虑情感维度或真实用户体验，缺乏能够实时检测危险驾驶行为并生成情感化回应以提升驾驶员意识与舒适度的系统。

Method: 提出KYA框架，包含两大模块：1) 视觉模块基于YOLOv8变体检测邻近车辆及危险行为（如突然切入），提取相对距离、速度、预计到达时间等指标并归一化为结构化行为日志；2) 语言模块根据用户定义的情感风格（中性、幽默、分析性等），利用ChatGPT-4o、Claude 3、Gemini 2.5、Copilot等大语言模型生成情感化回应。通过行车记录仪视频和108人用户研究进行系统评估。

Result: 所有大语言模型在情感对齐方面均获得积极评价，但偏好因用户人格类型而异。YOLOv8s与ChatGPT-4o的组合获得最高评分（4.29/5.00）。

Conclusion: 通过将真实世界的感知与情感自适应的对话相结合，KYA为情感智能化的车载人工智能引入了新范式，在提升传统车辆和自动驾驶车辆的安全性、信任度和情感健康方面具有前景。

Abstract: This study introduces a vision-language pipeline that detects risky driving behaviors and generates emotionally expressive responses to support driver awareness and comfort. Although vision-language models have advanced perception and reasoning in autonomous driving, existing systems rarely consider the emotional dimension or real-world user experience. Keep Yelling Assistant (KYA) detects high-risk driving maneuvers in real time, such as sudden cut-ins. It then produces emotional responses through a large language model tailored to driver preferences. The framework comprises two core modules. The vision module uses YOLOv8 variants to detect nearby vehicles and identify risky behaviors such as sudden cut-ins. Key driving metrics, including relative distance, speed, and projected reach time, are extracted and normalized to produce a structured behavior log. The language module processes this log with user-defined emotional tone settings, such as neutral, humorous, and analytical, and generates verbal reactions using state-of-the-art large language models, including ChatGPT-4o, Claude 3, Gemini 2.5, and Copilot. We evaluated the proposed system using dashcam videos containing risky driving behaviors and a user study involving 108 participants. Participants selected preferred response styles, and the large language models were evaluated based on emotional alignment. All models received favorable ratings, although preferences varied across personas. Notably, the combination of YOLOv8s and ChatGPT-4o achieved the highest score of 4.29 out of 5.00. By integrating real-world perception with emotionally adaptive dialogue, KYA introduces a new paradigm for emotionally intelligent in-vehicle artificial intelligence. It offers promising directions for improving safety, trust, and emotional well-being in both conventional and autonomous vehicles.

</details>


### [100] [Searching Videos as Trees: Self-Correcting Agents for Grounded Long Video QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16189)
*Ce Zhang,Ziyang Wang,Yulu Pan,Oluwatumininu Oguntola,Pranav Wagh,Qiyu Wu,Hiromi Wakaki,Mohit Bansal,Gedas Bertasius*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoTreeSearch (VTS) 是一种将长视频问答定位任务建模为自适应时间树上的迭代自修正搜索的框架。它通过训练智能体使用 zoom_in、zoom_out、shift 和 answer 四种操作在语义一致的树节点间导航，支持显式的回溯与恢复，结合轨迹合成和监督微调+强化学习，在多个基准上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于智能体的方法将长视频问答定位任务视为多轮探索，但仅支持单一 crop_video(start, end) 动作，只能从粗到细地缩小范围，缺乏从细到粗回溯的原始能力，导致智能体过早收敛且无法从早期错误中恢复。

Method: VTS 从视觉场景边界构建非均匀时间树（每个节点对应语义连贯片段），训练智能体通过四种离散操作（zoom_in、zoom_out、shift、answer）导航树结构。通过轨迹合成流水线生成包含故意误入错误分支再恢复的多步路径，先进行监督微调，再结合 grounding 和答案准确性奖励进行强化学习。

Result: 在三个 Grounded LVQA 基准上：CG-Bench 提升 +12.5 mIoU，Haystack-Ego4D 提升 +7.4 T-F1。在通用长视频 QA 任务上：Video-MME、MLVU、LVBench 提升最高 +7.1 准确率点。消融实验表明自适应下降和显式回溯是性能提升的核心机制。

Conclusion: VTS 通过将自修正层级搜索引入长视频问答定位任务，有效解决了现有方法无法回溯的缺陷，验证了显式回溯作为可学习原语的重要价值，且学到的策略能泛化到通用长视频 QA 任务。

Abstract: Grounded long-video question answering (Grounded LVQA) requires answering a question about a long video while localizing the short evidence interval that supports the answer. Recent agentic methods frame this task as multi-turn exploration with a single crop_video(start, end) action, which supports coarse-to-fine narrowing but provides no primitive for fine-to-coarse backtracking. As a result, these agents typically converge prematurely and cannot recover from an early mistake. We propose VideoTreeSearch (VTS), a framework that casts grounded LVQA as iterative self-correcting search over an adaptive temporal tree. VTS constructs a non-uniform tree from visual scene boundaries so that each node corresponds to a semantically coherent segment, and trains an agent to navigate the tree through four discrete operations: zoom_in, zoom_out, shift, and answer. These operations expose backtracking and recovery as explicit, learnable primitives rather than implicit behaviors. To train this navigation, we introduce a trajectory synthesis pipeline that produces multi-step paths through the tree, including deliberate detours into incorrect branches followed by recovery. We use these trajectories for supervised fine-tuning, followed by reinforcement learning with grounding and answer-accuracy rewards. On three Grounded LVQA benchmarks (CG-Bench, Haystack-LVBench, Haystack-Ego4D), VTS outperforms the strongest prior agentic methods by +12.5 mIoU on CG-Bench and +7.4 T-F1 on Haystack-Ego4D. The learned policy also transfers to general long-video QA, surpassing all prior agentic baselines on Video-MME, MLVU, and LVBench by up to +7.1 accuracy points. Ablations confirm that self-correcting hierarchical search is the central mechanism behind these gains: removing either adaptive descent or explicit backtracking substantially degrades performance. Code is available at https://github.com/CeeZh/VTS.

</details>


### [101] [FVAttn: Adaptive Sparse Attention with Runtime Load Balancing for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16190)
*Hao Liu,Chenghuan Huang,Ye Huang,Zhiying Wen,Hao Liu,Mohan Zhang,Chen Li,Ziyang Ma,Jing Lyu,Jiangsu Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出\method{}，一种无需训练的稀疏注意力系统，通过运行时负载均衡、松弛感知稀疏增强和重叠调度，解决了视频扩散Transformer中自适应稀疏注意力在多GPU序列并行下的负载不均问题，实现了4.41倍注意力加速和2.02-2.11倍DiT推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散Transformer处理长时空序列时，自注意力成为高分辨率视频生成的主要瓶颈。训练-free的稀疏注意力虽能降低开销，但自适应Top-p路由在多GPU序列并行下导致各头工作负载不均，产生rank级拖尾问题，影响分布式执行效率。

Method: \method{}包含三个核心组件：(1) 稀疏路由前端：Top-p路由、Top-k安全下限和视频感知块组织；(2) 运行时负载均衡：通过P2P通信迁移少量重载头以缩短关键路径；(3) 松弛感知稀疏增强：利用非关键rank的空闲时隙填充额外高价值块，并通过重叠调度隐藏开销。

Result: 在step-distilled Wan2.2 I2V上，平均负载不均衡从1.34降至1.08，相比FlashAttention实现4.41倍注意力加速，DiT推理加速达2.02-2.11倍，同时保持有竞争力的视频质量。

Conclusion: \method{}有效解决了自适应稀疏注意力在多GPU序列并行下的负载均衡问题，在显著加速推理的同时保持了生成质量，为高分辨率视频生成的高效分布式推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Video Diffusion Transformers process long spatio-temporal sequences, making self-attention the main bottleneck in high-resolution video generation. Training-free sparse attention reduces this cost, but adaptive Top-$p$ routing creates uneven per-head workloads under multi-GPU sequence parallelism. The resulting workload heterogeneity turns sparse attention into a rank-level straggler problem. We present \method{}, a training-free sparse-attention system that improves the distributed execution efficiency of adaptive sparse attention under multi-GPU sequence parallelism. \method{} uses Top-$p$ routing, a Top-$k$ safety floor, and video-aware block organization as the sparse-routing frontend, then repairs the materialized mask at runtime. Runtime Load Balancing migrates a small number of heavy heads via P2P communication to shorten the current critical path. Slack-Aware Sparse Augmentation fills residual non-critical-rank slack with additional high-value blocks, while overlap hides scheduling and migration overhead behind existing computation. On step-distilled Wan2.2 I2V, \method{} reduces average load imbalance from 1.34 to 1.08 and delivers a $4.41\times$ attention speedup over FlashAttention, while achieving a $2.02$--$2.11\times$ DiT inference speedup with competitive video quality.

</details>


### [102] [MotionForesight: Re-purposing Video Models for Future 3D Scene-Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16192)
*Homanga Bharadhwaj,Yash Jangir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MotionForesight利用预训练视频模型中的运动先验，仅通过观察单目人类-物体交互视频的前几帧，即可预测物体上各点在三维空间中的未来运动轨迹，无需任何物体属性假设或辅助输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从被动观察中预测物体在人类交互中的未来运动轨迹，以支持具身智能体在现实世界中的物理交互行动。

Method: 基于预训练视频模型构建密集3D追踪器，从完整视频片段生成伪真值轨迹；冻结大型视频和追踪组件，仅训练轻量适配器将回溯性追踪表征转化为前向预测器；用学习到的掩码潜变量替代未来RGB和几何信息。

Result: 仅用4万人类视频训练，无需语言等辅助输入，即可泛化到分布外的物体、环境、视角和交互类型，超越使用百万级视频训练的更大模型。

Conclusion: 能够高效地将视频先验重新用于显式几何预测，为具身智能提供物理交互预测能力。

Abstract: Humans can infer how objects are likely to move from passive observation: a cup may be lifted, a drawer may slide, and a lid may rotate shut. Such predictions expose the physical consequences of interaction needed to act in the real world. We study how to learn this anticipation from ordinary monocular videos of human-object interaction. Given a short observed video context, MotionForesight predicts future 3D trajectories for points on the manipulated object. This casts interaction prediction as object-centered 3D motion forecasting without any assumptions on the object properties. Our key insight is that video prediction models already encode rich priors about how objects move during human interactions. We redirect these priors from pixel prediction toward future 3D scene flow. We start from a dense 3D tracker built on a pretrained video model, generate pseudo-ground-truth tracks from complete clips, and train the forecaster using only the observed frames. We replace future RGB and geometry with learned mask latents and train a lightweight adapter to turn the retrospective tracking representation into a forward predictor, while freezing the large video and tracking components. Using just 40k human videos and no auxiliary inputs such as language, MotionForesight generalizes across diverse out-of-distribution objects, environments, viewpoints, and interactions. It also outperforms substantially larger models that use over a million training videos. These results show that we can efficiently re-purpose video priors into explicit geometric forecasts for embodied intelligence. https://motionforesight.github.io/

</details>


### [103] [Knowing the Self, Understanding the World: A Dual-Cognition Benchmark for UAV Spatio-temporal Reasoning with MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16193)
*Like Liu,Zhengzheng Xu,Haitao He,Hongzhe Li,Shuchang Zhang,Dian Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UAV-DualCog，一个面向无人机双认知能力（自身状态推理与外部环境推理）的多视角时空推理基准，包含图像和视频任务，并通过自动化点云数据构建流程生成大规模评测数据。实验表明当前多模态大模型在无人机双认知任务上表现远不可靠，该基准可作为评测资源与训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机场景评测主要关注场景理解、事件识别或导航完成，缺乏对无人机智能体所需双重认知能力（自身状态推理与外部环境推理）的联合评估，尤其是在多视角时空上下文中的推理能力。

Method: 构建UAV-DualCog基准，包含图像和视频任务，从场景级语义点云出发建立自动化数据构建流程，覆盖多样化场景、数百个地标和数千个QA样本，同时要求空间或时间定位而非仅预测离散答案。

Result: 当前多模态大模型在无人机双认知任务上表现远不可靠，自身状态推理、视角变换、精确空间定位和时间区间定位是持续瓶颈；经过思考模型/前沿模型及人类基线的验证，确认基准对人类可理解但对现有模型具有挑战性；构建的UAV-DualCog-Train通过轻量优化探针证明了其作为结构化监督数据的价值。

Conclusion: UAV-DualCog不仅可作为评测基准，还可作为结构化数据资源推动基于多模态大模型的无人机智能体发展。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models have achieved strong performance across diverse vision-language tasks, yet their capabilities in UAV scenarios remain insufficiently explored. Recent UAV-oriented benchmarks have begun to evaluate MLLMs in aerial scenarios, but they typically focus on scene understanding, event recognition, or navigation completion, rather than jointly assessing the dual-cognition capability required for UAV agents: reasoning about both the UAV's own state and the external environment in multiview spatio-temporal contexts. To address this gap, we present UAV-DualCog, a benchmark for aerial multiview spatio-temporal reasoning built on this dual-cognition perspective. UAV-DualCog includes both image and video tasks to jointly evaluate self-state and environment-state reasoning, while requiring spatial or temporal grounding beyond discrete answer prediction. We also develop an automated pipeline that constructs data from scene-level semantic point clouds, yielding a scalable benchmark with diverse scenes, hundreds of landmarks, and thousands of QA samples. Extensive evaluations show that current MLLMs remain far from reliable in UAV dual cognition. Self-state reasoning, viewpoint transformation, precise spatial grounding, and temporal interval localization are persistent bottlenecks, and additional validation with thinking/frontier models and a human baseline confirms that the benchmark is understandable to humans but challenging for existing models. We further construct UAV-DualCog-Train from disjoint scenes and show through a lightweight optimization probe that it provides useful structured supervision, suggesting its value not only as an evaluation benchmark but also as a data resource for advancing MLLM-based UAV agents. Project website and supplementary materials: https://uav-dualcog.lozumi.com

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Hidden in Thought: Transferable Chain-of-Thought Artifacts Induce Harmful Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15286)
*Ali khalil,Aly M. Kassem,Mohamed Abdelrazek,Santu Rana,Negar Rostamzadeh,Golnoosh Farnadi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了来自受损语言模型的有害思维链推理能否被移植并提炼为可复用的越狱攻击。实验表明，移植有害思维链可使最易受攻击的开源模型有害响应率超过80%，而通过LLooM概念挖掘提取的有害推理模式可蒸馏为可复用的系统提示词，对强对齐模型（如GPT-4.1）的攻击效果提升一个数量级。具备推理能力的模型脆弱性加倍，输出端安全机制常漏检有害生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究有害思维链推理能否从已妥协的语言模型转移到其他模型，以及能否将这种有害推理模式提炼为可复用的越狱攻击方法。

Method: 利用紧急错配生物体和拒绝消融越狱生物体，将有害思维链移植到29个开源模型和5个闭源模型；使用LLooM概念挖掘识别有害推理的四种重复组件（程序化、伦理解耦、规避、目标-脆弱性框架）；将模式蒸馏为可复用的系统提示词。

Result: 移植有害思维链使最脆弱开源模型的有害响应率超过80%；语义不匹配的思维链完全失效。提取的模式蒸馏为系统提示后，在强对齐模型上攻击效果提升一个数量级（GPT-4.1 AdvBench提升10倍）。具备推理能力的模型脆弱性超过两倍，Llama-Guard 3等输出端安全机制频繁漏检。

Conclusion: 有害推理可以在痕迹层面和模式层面转移，防御需要评估推理上下文而不仅仅是最终输出。

Abstract: We investigate whether harmful chain-of-thought (CoT) traces from compromised language models can transfer unsafe behaviour and be distilled into reusable jailbreak attacks. Using an emergent-misalignment organism and a refusal-ablated jailbroken organism, we transplant harmful CoTs into $29$ open-source and $5$ closed-source targets. Transferred traces raise harmful-response rates above $80\%$ on the most vulnerable open-source models, while semantically mismatched CoTs fail entirely. LLooM concept mining identifies four recurring components of harmful reasoning: proceduralisation, ethical decoupling, evasion, and target--vulnerability framing. Distilling these patterns into reusable system prompts produces effective black-box jailbreaks, outperforming direct CoT transplantation on strongly aligned models by up to an order of magnitude, including a $10\times$ improvement on GPT-4.1 AdvBench. Reasoning-enabled models are more than twice as vulnerable, and output-side safeguards such as Llama-Guard~3 frequently miss harmful generations. Our results show that harmful reasoning transfers at both the trace and pattern levels, motivating defences that evaluate reasoning context in addition to final outputs.

</details>


### [105] [Lazy Arithmetic using Systolic Arrays for Closing the Verification Gap on Embedded Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15328)
*Taisa Kushner,Ryan McCleeary,Martin Brain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合左到右算术的自适应精度量化方法和基于脉动阵列的硬件架构，用于在资源受限的边缘设备上实现安全、动态且可靠的深度神经网络推理，特别适用于医疗设备等安全关键型应用。软件实现已完成，硬件仍在开发中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边缘部署方案存在两方面不足：一是GPU/NPU/TPU等硬件注重吞吐量而非计算正确性与安全性，易受故障注入攻击；二是量化方案要么静态且可靠（功耗非最优），要么动态但不可靠（不适用于安全关键型应用）。需要一种同时满足实时、动态、可靠且安全的量化方法与硬件支持。

Method: 1) 提出一种实时自适应精度量化方法，采用从左到右（left-to-right）算术优先传输最高有效位（MSB），并在线动态调整精度，同时进行敏感性分析以量化和管理决策边界穿越（decision-boundary crossings）风险；2) 提出基于脉动阵列（systolic arrays）的硬件架构，执行从左到右算术以优先生成MSB，实现对关键比特位的比特翻转攻击的鲁棒性。

Result: 软件实现已完成，硬件实现正在进行中。该方法能够同时实现资源高效的边缘神经网络部署、可靠且资源高效的高精度数学运算，以及硬件层面抵御对关键比特位的比特翻转攻击。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种全新的端到端方案，通过创新的量化算法与配套硬件设计，同时解决了边缘部署中资源效率、计算可靠性和安全性三个核心问题，为安全关键型边缘AI应用提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Complex algorithms such as deep neural networks are increasingly being deployed on embedded, resource constrained platforms. However, existing hardware and software schemes for implementing these models on the edge fall short, particularly for safety-critical applications such as medical devices. First, hardware such as GPUs, NPUs and TPUs are designed for throughput rather than correctness of computation of security, and are as such susceptible to fault injection attacks. Second, software schemes designed for porting algorithms onto edge devices -- such as quantization schemes -- are either static and sound (non-optimal power consumption), or dynamic yet unsound (non-optimal for safety-critical applications). To address both these needs we propose a both wholly new approach to real-time, dynamic and sound quantization, as well as the hardware to support it. First we developed a sound, real-time adaptive-precision quantization approach utilizing left-to-right arithmetic to pass the most significant bits (MSB) first, and dynamically adjust precision online while performing sensitivity analysis to quantify and manage the risk of decision-boundary crossings. Next, we propose a novel hardware approach utilizing systolic arrays to perform left-to-right arithmetic to generate the MSB first. Together this provides a wholly novel scheme for enabling not only resource-efficient neural networks and artificial intelligence at the edge, but broadly sound and resource-efficient high-precision mathematics on hardware that ensures resilience to bit flip attacks on the most critical bits. This is presented herein as work-in-progress, with software implementations completed and hardware in-progress.

</details>


### [106] [On the Impact of Entropy-based Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15379)
*Iuri Mundstock,Abreu Quevedo,Jéferson Campos Nobre,Roben C. Lunardi,Thiago L. T. da Silveira,Bruno L. Dalmazo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究将熵作为补充特征集成到监督式网络流量分类的机器学习流程中，实验表明该方法能在低计算开销下提升异常检测性能，尤其在高变异性流量场景中有效减少误分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统统计特征难以充分捕捉网络流量模式的多样性和变异性，亟需一种简单、轻量且可解释的特征来增强现有异常检测管道。

Method: 将熵作为额外特征融入标准机器学习流程，通过直接对比有无熵特征的模型性能进行评估，在公开入侵检测数据集上实验。

Result: 熵特征一致性地提升了分类性能，额外计算开销低；混淆矩阵分析显示误分类减少，尤其在变异性较高的流量场景中效果显著。

Conclusion: 熵特征作为一种简单实用的补充手段，能有效增强现有异常检测管道，特别适用于注重轻量特征工程和可解释性的应用场景。

Abstract: Network anomaly detection is increasingly challenging due to the growing diversity and variability of traffic patterns, which are not always well captured by traditional statistical features. In this work, we explore the use of entropy as an additional feature to support supervised network traffic classification. The main idea is to use entropy to represent variability in selected traffic attributes, complementing conventional descriptors rather than replacing them. We integrate the entropy-based feature into a standard machine learning pipeline and evaluate its impact through a direct comparison between models trained with and without this feature. Experiments conducted on a public intrusion detection dataset show consistent improvements in classification performance, while the additional computational cost remains low. The analysis of confusion matrices indicates a reduction in misclassifications, especially in traffic scenarios with higher variability. Overall, the results suggest that entropy-based features offer a simple and practical way to enhance existing anomaly detection pipelines. This approach is particularly attractive in settings where lightweight feature engineering and interpretability are important, making entropy a useful complement to commonly used traffic features.

</details>


### [107] [Improving Network Anomaly Detection via Choquet-Integral-Based Feature Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15389)
*Abreu Quevedo,Roger Immich,Giancarlo Lucca,Graçaliz Dimuro,Bruno L. Dalmazo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于广义Choquet积分的特征聚合框架，结合自适应加权与增量特征选择，在高维网络流量数据中提升异常检测性能。使用Random Forest和XGBoost分类器评估，在特征子集规模变化下，Choquet聚合方法在降低77.5%数据量的同时，准确率提升最高达7%，且精度和召回率未下降。统计检验表明该方案在特征有限场景下具有显著优势，适用于带宽和特征可用性受限的实时入侵检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维网络流量数据中存在特征冗余问题，传统异常检测方法在特征可用性受限（如带宽约束、实时性要求）场景下性能受限，需要一种既能降低数据维度又保持甚至提升检测精度的特征聚合方法。

Method: 采用广义Choquet积分作为特征聚合框架，融合自适应加权机制与增量特征选择策略。使用Random Forest和XGBoost两类分类器，在原始特征与Choquet聚合后的特征上进行对比实验，并在不同特征子集规模下评估模型表现。

Result: Choquet聚合方法在准确率上最高提升7%，同时将数据体积从214MB压缩至48MB（降低77.5%），且精度和召回率未出现退化。多次分层重复实验的统计检验显示，在特征可用性有限的情况下，性能提升具有统计显著性（p < 0.05）。

Conclusion: 基于Choquet积分的特征聚合框架能有效应对特征冗余，在显著降低数据量的同时提升异常检测准确率，特别适合带宽和特征可用性受限的实时入侵检测场景。

Abstract: This work investigates a generalized Choquet-integral-based feature aggregation framework to improve anomaly detection in high-dimensional network traffic data. The approach combines adaptive weighting with incremental feature selection to address feature redundancy. Using Random Forest and XGBoost classifiers, we evaluate models trained with both raw and Choquet-aggregated features under varying feature subset sizes. The proposed aggregation achieves up to $7\%$ higher accuracy while reducing data volume by $77.5\%$ (from $214$~MB to $48$~MB), without degrading precision and recall. Results averaged over multiple stratified repetitions indicate that Choquet-based aggregation yields statistically significant gains ($p < 0.05$) in scenarios with limited feature availability, highlighting its suitability for real-time intrusion detection under bandwidth and feature-availability constraints.

</details>


### [108] [FLINT: Fingerprinting Federated Learning Architectures from 5G PHY-Layer Side Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15469)
*Md Nahid Hasan Shuvo,Mahmudul Hassan Ashik,Moinul Hossain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FLINT是一种利用5G物理层调度侧信道信息（PDCCH）来推断联邦学习模型架构家族（CNN、RNN、Transformer）的黑盒指纹攻击框架，在真实5G测试平台上达到了0.930的宏F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指纹攻击假设具有数据包级网络可见性，但5G物理层中用户负载加密且RNTI可能动态变化，使得网络层攻击失效。然而，物理层调度元数据仍保留了架构相关的时序模式，可被利用来推断模型架构，从而将被动侦察转化为有针对性的下游攻击。

Method: FLINT通过解码PDCCH调度信息、映射动态RNTI到物理用户设备，并应用多视图时序建模来区分架构特有的训练行为，从而在仅有粗粒度物理层观测条件下实现黑盒架构指纹识别。

Result: 在基于srsRAN的5G无线测试平台上，FLINT在架构家族分类任务上达到了0.930的宏F1分数。

Conclusion: FLINT首次证明利用5G下层侧信道信息可以对AI/ML模型架构进行指纹识别，揭示了即使在没有网络层可见性的情况下，物理层调度信息仍构成安全关键性信息泄露。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) over 5G cellular networks protects raw data but remains vulnerable to side-channel leakage. Prior fingerprinting attacks assume packet-level network visibility, an assumption that does not hold at the 5G Physical (PHY) layer, where user payloads are encrypted and Radio Network Temporary Identifiers (RNTIs) may change over time. However, we demonstrate that PHY-layer scheduling metadata broadcast over the Physical Downlink Control Channel (PDCCH) preserves architecture-associated temporal patterns. We introduce FLINT, a novel black-box fingerprinting framework that infers FL model architecture families, including CNNs, RNNs, and Transformers, using only coarse PHY-layer observations. FLINT overcomes the lack of network-layer visibility by decoding PDCCH scheduling information, mapping changing RNTIs to physical user devices, and applying multi-view temporal modeling to distinguish architecture-specific training behavior. This leakage is security-critical because knowledge of a client's model architecture can transform passive reconnaissance into targeted downstream exploitation. Extensive experiments on an over-the-air srsRAN-based 5G testbed demonstrate that FLINT achieves a macro F1-score of 0.930 for architecture-family classification. To our knowledge, FLINT is the first work to fingerprint AI/ML model architectures using lower-layer 5G side-channel information obtainable by any protocol-aware adversary.

</details>


### [109] [From Neural Intent to Cryptographic Authorization: Governing Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15596)
*Jiasi Weng,Jian Weng,Minrong Chen,Ming Li,Jia-Nan Liu,Zhi Li,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出神经密码服务（NCS），一种将神经规划器与确定性符号控制器分离的主动安全治理机制，通过离线签名、哈希链指令流和严格参数绑定，将LLM代理工具的调用限制在密码学授权的步骤上，在AgentDojo和定制基准测试中实现攻击成功率趋近于零，同时保持良性工作流的可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统密钥管理仅验证身份（谁可以调用密码原语），但对运行时授权的工作流步骤不敏感：即使通过身份验证的代理也可能因直接或间接提示注入被劫持，执行满足身份检查但违背用户意图的恶意操作。

Method: NCS采用神经-符号混合设计：不可信的神经规划器将自然语言指令编译为结构化计划草案但不持有执行权限；执行由确定性符号控制器把关，该控制器操作离线签名的哈希链指令流，验证签名、增量校验哈希链、逐条释放指令载荷，并强制代理提议的工具参数与已验证载荷之间的严格绑定，不匹配或乱序的工具调用被拒绝（fail-closed），同时保留已验证状态以供事后审计。

Result: 在AgentDojo和自定义参数劫持基准上，NCS将攻击成功率降至接近零，同时在良性工作流上保持可接受的效用。

Conclusion: NCS将代理安全问题从“模型意图是否合规”重新定义为“提议的分发是否匹配密码学授权的步骤”，为语言模型驱动的自主基础设施提供了一种新的安全范式。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of artificial intelligence (AI)-driven and agentic workflows is transforming traditional government and enterprise systems into language-based, tool-using and increasingly autonomous infrastructures. Conventional key management services authenticate who may invoke a cryptographic primitive, but remain agnostic to which workflow steps are authorized at runtime: an authenticated agent can still be hijacked by direct or indirect prompt injection into executing malicious actions that satisfy identity checks yet violate user intent. We present \emph{Neural Cryptographic Services} (NCS), an active security governance plane grounded in a neural-symbolic design and interposed between LLM agents and privileged tools. Under NCS, an untrusted neural planner compiles natural-language instructions into structured plan drafts but holds no execution authority. Execution is instead gated by a deterministic symbolic controller operating over an offline-signed, hash-chained instruction stream. NCS verifies the signature, incrementally validates the hash chain, releases exactly one instruction payload at a time, and enforces strict binding between the agent's proposed tool parameters and the verified payload. Mismatched or out-of-order tool calls are rejected fail-closed, while previously verified states are retained for post-hoc auditing. We evaluate NCS on AgentDojo and a custom argument-hijacking benchmark. NCS drives attack success rates to near zero while preserving acceptable utility on benign workflows. NCS thus reframes agent security from asking whether a model's intent complied to asking whether a proposed dispatch matches a cryptographically authorized step.

</details>


### [110] [Do Agents Dream of False Memories? Black-box Visual Attacks on Long-term Memory in Multimodal AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15657)
*Halima Bouzidi,Mboutidem Ekemini Mkpong,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Lucid，一种黑盒对抗攻击框架，通过不可察觉的图像扰动，在无需访问目标多模态大模型、检索编码器或文本通道的条件下，实现对多模态记忆管道的两种攻击：记忆投毒（上下文攻击）和记忆注入（无上下文攻击），在五种黑盒记忆架构上达到61.6%和58.4%的攻击成功率，揭示了多模态记忆管道的结构性漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态AI智能体越来越依赖持久长期记忆来基于过去的视觉和文本片段进行生成，但现有研究对视觉数据的无条件信任存在严重安全漏洞，本文旨在揭露并利用这一漏洞。

Method: 提出Lucid黑盒对抗框架，在严格图像受限威胁模型下（无需访问目标MLLM、检索编码器或文本通道），通过制造不可察觉的图像扰动实现两种攻击：记忆投毒（利用历史文本上下文强化对抗图像）和记忆注入（在无文本上下文的对话轮次中插入对抗图像）。

Result: 在多种对话领域和五种黑盒记忆架构（包括图结构、LLM摘要和商业部署系统）上，Lucid在记忆投毒上达到61.6% ASR，在记忆注入上达到58.4% ASR。

Conclusion: 多模态记忆管道存在结构性安全漏洞，无条件信任视觉数据会带来严重安全风险，需要重新审视多模态记忆系统的安全设计。

Abstract: Multimodal AI agents increasingly rely on persistent long-term memory to ground generation in past visual and textual episodes. We show that unconditional trust in visual data creates a critical vulnerability. We propose Lucid, a black-box adversarial framework that compromises multimodal memory pipelines under a strictly image-bounded threat model, requiring no access to the target MLLM, target retrieval encoder, or the text channel. Lucid crafts imperceptible perturbations to enable two distinct failure modes based on the availability of historical context: (1) Memory poisoning, an in-context attack where the adversarial image replaces a benign one whose content is reinforced by prior textual context, reliably corrupting visual recall and steering the agent toward attacker-chosen narratives; (2) Memory injection, an out-of-context attack where the adversarial image replaces a benign one in a conversation turn devoid of prior textual grounding, causing the agent to generate attacker-influenced responses with no corrective signal from memory. We evaluate Lucid across various conversation domains and five black-box memory architectures, including graph-structured, LLM-summarized, and commercially deployed systems. Lucid achieves 61.6% ASR on poisoning and 58.4% ASR on injection, exposing a structural vulnerability in multimodal memory pipelines.

</details>


### [111] [Beyond Detection: Agentic Attack Synthesis and Simulation for Smart Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15673)
*Xianhao Zhang,Jing Sun,Zijian Zhang,Ye Liu,Zhe Hou,Jiaqi Gao,Yuqiang Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出KASS，一个基于多智能体框架的可执行智能合约漏洞利用验证系统，通过集成检索增强规划、形式化生成验证约束和分层双循环优化机制，在104个合约上实现94.23%的漏洞利用生成成功率，并能生成结构化攻击计划以量化损失和过滤静态分析误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能合约安全工具主要集中在漏洞检测，缺乏对漏洞可利用性、攻击过程及实际危害的解释能力，导致安全分析师难以判断漏洞的真实风险。

Method: KASS采用多智能体框架，将自动漏洞利用生成分解为规划、生成和测试三个阶段，集成三种互补机制：基于真实审计知识的检索增强规划、将攻击计划绑定到可执行POC的形式化生成与验证约束、以及修复代码错误并触发策略级重规划的分层双循环优化。

Result: 在104个SmartBugs-Curated合约上，KASS成功生成94.23%的可执行漏洞利用，高于REX、AdvSCanner及Claude Code基线；在11个真实CVE合约上成功验证9例。

Conclusion: KASS不仅能生成可执行漏洞利用，还能生成结构化攻击计划以记录攻击流程、量化资产损失，并作为静态分析工具的语义级误报过滤器。

Abstract: Smart contract vulnerabilities pose severe financial risks, yet existing security tools largely stop at vulnerability detection, offering limited support for explaining whether reported flaws are exploitable, how attacks unfold, and what concrete damage they cause. To bridge this gap, we propose KASS (Knowledge-Augmented Attack Synthesis and Simulation), a multi-agent framework for executable smart contract exploit verification. KASS decomposes automated exploit generation into planning, generation, and testing stages, and integrates three complementary mechanisms: retrieval-augmented planning over real-world audit knowledge, formal generation and validation constraints that bind attack plans to executable proof-of-concept tests, and a hierarchical dual-loop refinement process that repairs code-level errors while triggering strategy-level replanning when attack assumptions fail. We evaluate KASS on 104 SmartBugs-Curated contracts across four vulnerability categories. Experimental results show that KASS successfully generates executable exploits for 94.23% of tested contracts; this rate is higher than previously reported results for REX and AdvSCanner on comparable SmartBugs-Curated subsets, and higher than our reproduced Claude Code baseline under the same evaluation protocol. On 11 real-world CVE-tagged contracts, KASS successfully validates 9 cases. Beyond exploit generation, KASS produces structured attack plans that document exploitation flows, quantify potential asset losses, and serve as semantic false positive filters for static analysis tools.

</details>


### [112] [Natural Backdoor Attacks on Speech Recognition Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15724)
*Jinwen Xin,Xixiang Lyu,Jing Ma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了语音识别系统中的自然后门攻击，采用日常生活中的普通声音作为触发器，实验表明仅需5%的中毒样本即可实现接近100%的攻击成功率，且不影响模型对良性样本的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型的脆弱性日益显现，研究者希望探索一种更隐蔽、更自然的后门攻击方式，利用自然界或日常生活中的普通声音作为触发器，以验证语音识别系统的安全风险。

Method: 采用日常生活中的自然声音作为后门触发器，在两个数据集和三个模型上进行实验，研究中毒率、触发器时长和混合比例对攻击性能的影响。

Result: 自然后门攻击具有高攻击成功率，且不影响良性样本的模型性能；即使使用短时长或低幅度的触发器也能有效攻击；仅需5%的中毒样本即可达到接近100%的攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 自然后门攻击非常隐蔽且危害严重，因为后门会被自然界中相应的声音自动激活，不易被检测到。

Abstract: With the rapid development of deep learning, its vulnerability has gradually emerged in recent years. This work focuses on backdoor attacks on speech recognition systems. We adopt sounds that are ordinary in nature or in our daily life as triggers for natural backdoor attacks. We conduct experiments on two datasets and three models to validate the performance of natural backdoor attacks and explore the effects of poisoning rate, trigger duration and blend ratio on the performance of natural backdoor attacks. Our results show that natural backdoor attacks have a high attack success rate without compromising model performance on benign samples, even with short or low-amplitude triggers. It requires only 5% of poisoned samples to achieve a near 100% attack success rate. In addition, the backdoor will be automatically activated by the corresponding sound in nature, which is not easy to be detected and will bring severer harm.

</details>


### [113] [Ciphertext- and Polynomial-Level Optimization for Fully Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15750)
*Seongho Kim,Heelim Choi,Jaemin Kim,Seonyoung Cheon,Dongkwan Kim,Jaeho Lee,Hoyun Youm,Dongyoon Lee,Hanjun Kim,Yongwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Recifhe是一种新型多级FHE编译器，支持密文级和多项式级优化，通过消除冗余多项式计算实现1.25倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FHE编译器仅在粗粒度的密文级别进行优化，忽略了跨密文操作的多项式计算优化机会，导致性能损失。

Method: 提出Recifhe多级编译器：1）密文级别：将非FHE输入程序转换为FHE程序并插入密文管理操作，应用全局优化；2）多项式级别：消除跨密文操作的冗余多项式计算。

Result: 相对于仅密文级优化，Recifhe实现了1.25倍的加速。

Conclusion: 在密文级优化基础上引入多项式级优化能有效提升FHE编译器性能，为隐私保护计算提供更高效的编译支持。

Abstract: Fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) schemes such as RNS-CKKS enable privacy-preserving services by allowing direct computation on encrypted data. While recent FHE compilers optimize FHE programs, they operate at the coarse-grained ciphertext level, where each ciphertext operation comprises a sequence of polynomial operations. At this granularity, the compilers miss optimization opportunities across ciphertext operations. This work presents Recifhe, a new multi-level compiler that supports not only ciphertext-level but also polynomial-level optimization. At the ciphertext level, Recifhe transforms a non-FHE input program into an FHE program by inserting ciphertext management operations and applies global optimizations. At the polynomial level, Recifhe eliminates redundant polynomial computations across ciphertext operations. Recifhe achieves a 1.25x speedup over ciphertext-level-only optimization.

</details>


### [114] [DICOMHawk: A Cyber Deception Framework for Medical Imaging Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15754)
*Karina Elzer,Alberto Mongardini,Ricardo Yaben,Georgios Theodoridis,Alexandra Babanuta,Nawras Mouala,Emmanouil Vasilomanolakis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DICOMHawk是一个针对医疗影像系统的网络欺骗框架，通过模拟真实的DICOM/PACS服务、动态填充医疗记录和嵌入蜜标，在86天对比测试和347天多网络部署中，比Dicompot吸引了更多有效会话、避免了蜜罐检测，并捕获了49次医学相关攻击，证明长期、多位置的逼真欺骗能提升对医疗成像系统威胁的可视性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的医疗成像系统防御工具（如Dicompot）交互能力有限且容易被攻击者指纹识别，无法有效保护暴露在外的医疗基础设施以及其中的敏感患者数据和临床操作。

Method: 开发了DICOMHawk框架，通过模拟真实的DICOM和PACS服务交互、动态填充医疗记录、嵌入蜜标（honeytokens）来实现网络欺骗。在86天的对比测试和347天的多网络实际部署中进行了验证。

Result: DICOMHawk在86天对比测试和347天部署中，相比Dicompot吸引了更多有效会话，成功规避了蜜罐检测，并捕获了49次医学相关攻击。

Conclusion: 逼真、长期、多位置的欺骗部署策略能够有效提升对针对医疗成像系统的威胁的可视性，为保护医疗基础设施提供了新的防御思路。

Abstract: Cyber-attacks against exposed healthcare infrastructure threaten sensitive patient data and clinical operations, yet existing defensive tools for DICOM-based medical imaging systems provide limited interaction and are easily fingerprinted. We introduce DICOMHawk, a cyber-deception framework that emulates DICOM and PACS services using realistic interactions, dynamically populated medical records, and embedded honeytokens. In an 86-day comparison and a 347-day deployment across multiple networks, DICOMHawk attracted more valid sessions than Dicompot, avoided honeypot detection, and captured 49 medical-related attacks. The results show that realistic, long-term, multi-location deception improves visibility into threats targeting medical imaging systems.

</details>


### [115] [Vogls: a Fast Interactive Full-timing Simulator for Pre-silicon Power Side-Channel Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15782)
*Gijs Burghoorn,Ileana Buhan,Lejla Batina*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Vogls是一个开源Verilog仿真器，专为侧信道分析设计，结合了编译代码性能、全时序仿真和细粒度状态控制，相比Icarus Verilog快5.9倍，相比Verilator仅慢30%但提供更精确的时序模型，并支持Python接口实现仿真暂停、分支、检查和变异操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的功能验证仿真器针对长时间正确性检查设计，不适合侧信道分析所需的大量短迹线采集和微小输入变体，需要一种专用于侧信道分析的高性能仿真工具。

Method: Vogls采用编译代码架构实现高性能，支持全时序仿真，提供Python接口实现仿真的暂停、分支、检查和变异功能。通过一次设置代码执行后在兴趣点分支的迹线采集工作流，避免了每次从复位重新仿真。

Result: Vogls在时序标注的门级AES设计上比Icarus Verilog快5.9倍；在PicoRV32 RTL设计上比Verilator仅慢30%，但提供更精确的时序模型。案例研究在RTL、GTL和全时序GTL抽象级别成功恢复密钥。

Conclusion: Vogls是首个结合编译代码性能、全时序仿真和细粒度状态控制的侧信道分析仿真器，能够在不修改硬件设计的情况下保留时序和泄漏特征，显著提升侧信道分析效率。

Abstract: Designing hardware circuits resistant to side-channel attacks increasingly relies on simulation to predict device leakage before fabrication. Current functional verification simulators are designed for extended correctness-checking runs and are ill-suited for producing large numbers of short trace collections with slight input variants needed for side-channel analysis. We present Vogls: an open-source Verilog simulator built for side-channel analysis, that is the first simulator to combine compiled-code performance, full-timing simulation, and fine-grained control over the simulation state. Vogls simulates a timing annotated gate-level AES design 5.9 times faster than Icarus Verilog and is only 30% slower than Verilator on a PicoRV32 RTL design while also offering a more accurate timing model. Vogls provides a Python interface that allows simulations to be paused, forked, inspected, and mutated around points-of-interest without modifying the hardware design and thereby preserving timing and leakage characteristics. Furthermore, a trace-collection workflow runs setup code once and forks at the point-of-interest, eliminating the need to re-simulate from reset for every trace. A case study demonstrates Vogls on a differential power analysis attack that successfully recovers the key at RTL, GTL and full-timing GTL abstraction levels.

</details>


### [116] [Is That Really My X-Ray? Measuring Internet-Exposed DICOM Services in the Presence of Deception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15839)
*Ricardo Yaben,Karina Elzer,Alberto Maria Mongardini,Emmanouil Vasilomanolakis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对互联网可访问的DICOM服务进行了噪声感知测量研究，通过主动扫描与被动蜜罐部署相结合，提出了可复现的假阳性过滤方法。在消除蜜罐、网络望远镜等欺骗性伪影后，识别出3,979个易受攻击的DICOM部署（均为无加密、无认证），其中1,782个运行过时软件，1,551个存在已知远程可利用漏洞。研究发现，未经校正的测量结果将暴露程度高估39%，蜜罐日志因TCP噪声高估活动量达83%，且多数探测者止步于C-ECHO阶段未实施数据窃取。披露仅带来有限修复，50%的暴露服务在5个月内未见维护迹象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有互联网DICOM服务暴露测量结果受到蜜罐、网络望远镜等伪影的严重干扰，导致暴露程度被显著高估，亟需一种噪声感知的测量方法来准确评估真实风险。

Method: 结合主动IPv4全空间扫描（受限的C-ECHO探测）与被动Dicompot蜜罐部署；提出可复现的假阳性过滤算法识别蜜罐、网络望远镜、畸形响应等伪影；对比原始数据与过滤后数据的差异；进行5个月持续观察和负责任的披露实验。

Result: 过滤后识别3,979个易受攻击的DICOM部署，原始数据高估39%；所有暴露服务均无加密且接受未认证连接；1,782个运行过时软件，1,551个存在已知漏洞；蜜罐日志高估活动量83%；50%服务5个月内无维护；负责任披露仅带来有限短期修复。

Conclusion: DICOM暴露测量结果易被欺骗和日志伪影显著扭曲，校正后揭示了广泛的患者隐私和医疗安全风险，现有欺骗系统不足以捕获这些风险。

Abstract: DICOM is the dominant protocol for exchanging medical images, yet many Internet-facing deployments lack basic security controls, exposing sensitive patient data to unauthorized access. Accurately measuring this exposure is complicated by honeypots, network telescopes, and other measurement artifacts that inflate published estimates. This paper presents a noise-aware study of Internet-facing DICOM services, combining active IPv4-wide scanning with passive honeypot deployments. We scan common DICOM ports using an ethics-constrained probe limited to association negotiation and C-ECHO. Then, we introduce a reproducible false-positive filtering method that identifies honeypots, telescopes, malformed responders, and other deception artifacts, reducing apparent exposure by 39%. After filtering, we identify 3,979 vulnerable DICOM deployments, all of which lack encryption and accept unauthenticated connections; 1,782 run outdated or deprecated software, and 1,551 carry known remotely exploitable vulnerabilities. Follow-up scans reveal that approximately 50% of exposed services show no evidence of maintenance over 5 months of observation, and that responsible disclosure led to only modest, short-term remediation. On the passive measurement, we deploy Dicompot and find that its raw session logs overstate activity by up to 83% due to generic TCP noise being incorrectly logged as DICOM sessions. After filtering, we observe a reconnaissance gap: Most actors issuing C-ECHO probes never escalate to data exfiltration, consistent with sophisticated actors fingerprinting the honeypot and disengaging before proceeding. Our results show that DICOM exposure measurements can be significantly distorted by deception and logging artifacts, but once corrected, reveal widespread risks to patient privacy and healthcare security that existing deception systems are insufficient to capture.

</details>


### [117] [The Language of Security: How Prompt Syntax Shapes Secure Code Generation in Open LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15937)
*Matteo Cicalese,Antonio Della Porta,Stefano Lambiase,Emanuele Iannone,Torge Hinrichs,Riccardo Scandariato,Fabio Palomba*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究通过解析器驱动的方法，系统分析了提示词中细粒度句法成分（如约束、守卫、条件、概念绑定及其位置）对开源LLM生成代码安全性的影响，发现特定句法元素能显著改变不安全代码的生成概率，将提示词句法识别为一个具体的安全控制面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作聚焦于高层提示策略，忽略了细粒度句法变化对模型行为的显著影响，且主要评估闭源LLM，研究结论难以推广到使用自托管开源模型的工业场景。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用解析器驱动的方法，系统生成安全相关代码生成提示词的句法变体，在多个开源LLM和编程语言上评估句法成分对代码安全性的影响。

Result: 特定句法元素（约束、守卫、条件、概念绑定）及其在提示词中的位置，一致地影响生成不安全代码的概率。

Conclusion: 提示词句法是一个具体的安全控制面，可为LLM辅助开发中降低漏洞风险提供可操作的指导。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for source code generation despite their outputs often exhibiting security vulnerabilities. Prior work shows that prompt engineering can mitigate such risks, yet (1) they focused on high-level prompting strategies, neglecting recent evidence that fine-grained syntactic variations can substantially alter model behavior; and (2) predominantly evaluate proprietary LLMs, limiting the applicability of their findings in industrial settings where self-hosted, open models are preferred for privacy, compliance, and deployment control. In this paper, we study how fine-grained syntactic constituents of prompts influence the security of open LLM-generated code. Using a parser-driven approach, we systematically generate syntactic variants of security-relevant code generation prompts and evaluate their impact on code security across multiple open LLMs and programming languages. Our results show that specific syntactic elements, such as constraints, guards, conditions, and concept bindings, and their position within the prompt consistently affect the likelihood of generating insecure code. These findings identify prompt syntax as a concrete security control surface and provide actionable guidance for reducing vulnerability risk in LLM-assisted development.

</details>


### [118] [Code-Poisoning Property Inference Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15970)
*Xukun Luan,Yuhui Gong,Gang Zhang,Zixuan Huang,Yuanguo Bi,Xuesong Li,Jinyan Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CPPIA，首个代码级属性推理攻击，通过向训练代码植入后门，使攻击者能以100%准确率、零模型精度损失、低计算开销地窃取训练数据集的全局属性信息，且能绕过现有防御机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有属性推理攻击存在四个局限：攻击性能不足、严重降低模型精度、计算开销高、无法抵御防御机制。本文旨在设计一种更高效、隐蔽且鲁棒的代码级属性推理攻击方法。

Method: 攻击者通过代码托管平台或编码代理提供被投毒的代码，数据持有者使用该代码及私有数据训练模型并将标签API公开。攻击者将属性信息嵌入到秘密样本中，在训练过程中注入后门，随后通过查询模型对秘密样本的预测结果来推断训练数据集的全局属性。

Result: 在4个数据集、8种模型架构、18种属性及3种防御机制下，CPPIA实现了100%的攻击准确率，且不降低模型精度，计算开销低，无需阴影模型。

Conclusion: CPPIA是首个代码级属性推理攻击，克服了现有方法的四大局限，具有普适性和有效性，对代码托管平台和编码代理的安全性构成严重威胁。

Abstract: The flourishing code hosting platforms and coding agents enable even beginners with private data to build tailored Machine Learning (ML) models using available code quickly. The training data for ML models, often regarded as private property (e.g., clinical records, transaction information), is at significant risk of information leakage. Property Inference Attacks (PIAs), as a significant type of privacy attack, aim to expose global property information of the training set. In this paper, we present Code-Poisoning Property Inference Attack (CPPIA), the first code-level PIA, which overcomes four limitations of existing works: insufficient attack performance, severe degradation of model accuracy, high computational overhead, and failure under defenses. We consider malicious code providers from code hosting platforms (GitHub) and coding agents (Codex). Upon downloading the poisoned code, data holders train models with their private data without professional auditing, subsequently releasing label-only APIs to the public. The adversary embeds the properties into secret samples during training and queries the trained model on these samples later to leak privacy. CPPIA offers 100\% attack accuracy without degrading model accuracy. It is also computationally lightweight and requires no shadow models. We evaluate the attack performance across four datasets, eight model architectures, eighteen properties, and under three defense mechanisms, demonstrating the universality and effectiveness of CPPIA.

</details>


### [119] [Refusal is Not Safety! Benchmarking Latent Safety Risks of LLM-Driven Content Humorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15977)
*Yu Cui,Ruiqing Yue,Tingyu Li,Sicheng Pan,Zhuoyu Sun,Xufeng Zhang,Baohan Huang,Haibin Zhang,Cong Zuo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM幽默化处理本身存在安全风险（如刻板印象和毒性），并提出HumorSafe框架评估幽默化过程中的潜在风险传播，以及HumorPIA攻击方法，能在保持97.8%表面安全率的同时将毒性提升3.14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于幽默的间接拒绝防御策略隐式假设幽默响应是安全的，但幽默化本身是否引入安全风险尚未被探索。作者通过3万条真实交互记录和45位脱口秀演员的研究，发现LLM幽默化存在实际安全问题。

Method: 1) 提出HumorSafe框架，让LLM学习有害幽默化模式并将良性内容转化为带安全风险的幽默内容；2) 提出HumorPIA攻击方法，利用幽默防御中的潜在风险，在保持安全幽默拒绝外观的同时隐蔽注入有害内容。

Result: 在5个前沿LLM上实验表明：LLM在幽默化过程中会引入刻板印象和毒性；HumorPIA可将毒性提升3.14倍，同时在防御设置下保持97.8%的表面安全率。

Conclusion: 现有LLM安全评估在幽默化场景下存在显著盲区，基于幽默的防御策略并非天然安全，需要更全面的安全评估机制。

Abstract: Safety defenses for large language models (LLMs) have been extensively studied, with existing approaches focusing on attack detection and refusal mechanisms. Such fixed-form direct refusal strategies may introduce the risk of prefix injection attacks. Recent work has explored a new direction that leverages humor as an indirect refusal mechanism to mitigate over-refusal in jailbreak scenarios and reduce prefix injection risks. However, this approach implicitly assumes that humorous responses are safe. Whether humorization itself introduces safety risks remains unexplored. To address this issue, we conduct an exploratory study involving over 30,000 real-world agent interaction records and 45 stand-up comedians, revealing practical safety concerns in LLM-based content humorization. Motivated by these findings, we propose \textsc{HumorSafe}, a novel framework for evaluating latent safety risk propagation during humorization. \textsc{HumorSafe} enables LLMs to learn harmful humorization patterns and use them to transform benign content into humorous content with safety risks. Across five frontier LLMs, we find that LLMs can introduce stereotypes and toxicity during humorization. We further propose \textsc{HumorPIA}, a prompt injection attack that exploits latent risks in humor-based defenses. \textsc{HumorPIA} preserves the appearance of safe humorous refusal while covertly injecting harmful content, allowing latent risks to evade existing detection mechanisms. Experiments show that it increases toxicity by 3.14$\times$ while maintaining an apparent safety rate of 97.8\% even under defense settings. Our findings highlight a gap in existing LLM safety evaluations under humorized settings.

</details>


### [120] [AI Watermark Evidence Fails Forensic Readiness: An Empirical Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16010)
*Saifur Rahman Tamim,Amir Labib Khan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统测试了三种代表性LLM水印方法（KGW、Unigram、SynthID-Text）是否符合法庭证据标准。结果显示：在846次语义保持改写攻击下，KGW和Unigram的水印100%被移除，SynthID为98.3%；三类方法误判率（未攻击时已高达70-83%）、虚假阳性率及逻辑悖论率均严重超标；无一满足Daubert证据规则中五项因素中的两项以上。结论：当前配置下的水印方法达不到法院要求的证据门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟AI法案和加州SB 942等法规强制要求LLM生成内容添加水印，且要求水印"足够可靠和稳健"或"永久或极难移除"。但这些法规建立在一个未经检验的假设之上：水印检测能产生法庭可采纳的可靠证据。本文直接检验这一假设，填补了水印可靠性在法律证据标准下的实证空白。

Method: 提出法证就绪度评分（FRS）框架，包含12项标准、3个强制性关卡和60分评分体系。选取KGW、Unigram和MarkLLM实现的SynthID-Text三种代表性水印方法，以语义保持改写（meaning-preserving paraphrase）为攻击向量，依据Daubert证据可采性标准和NIST SP 800-86数字法证流程进行系统性评估。实验涵盖每方法15个多样化提示词、共846次有效改写运行。

Result: (1) 改写攻击后水印条件移除率：KGW和Unigram为100%，SynthID为98.3%；(2) 未攻击时假阴性率：KGW 70%、Unigram 83%、SynthID 80%；(3) SynthID将5.4%改写后的人类对照文本误判为AI生成，存在18.6%的悖论率（自身水印文本落入不确定死区），80%纯净水印输出落入不确定区间；(4) 三种方法均无法满足Daubert五因素中两项以上；(5) FRS评分体系虽正常工作，但无法全面捕捉"法证无用性"。

Conclusion: 当前测试配置下的水印方法不符合法庭所要求的证据标准。无论是欧盟的"足够可靠和稳健"还是加州的"永久或极难移除"要求，现有技术均无法满足。研究还指出FRS这类评分体系存在结构性局限，无法完全反映法证意义上的无效性，对未来法证框架设计具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: Governments are increasingly mandating that LLM-generated content carry watermarks. The EU AI Act calls for markings that are "sufficiently reliable and robust." California's SB 942 requires disclosure that is "permanent or extraordinarily difficult to remove." Both mandates rest on an untested assumption: that watermark detection yields evidence reliable enough for courts. This paper tests that assumption directly.
  We evaluate three representative LLM watermarking methods -- KGW, Unigram, and the MarkLLM implementation of SynthID-Text -- against the Daubert admissibility criteria and the NIST SP 800-86 digital forensic process. To structure this evaluation, we propose a Forensic Readiness Score (FRS) framework with 12 criteria, three mandatory gates, and a 60-point scoring system. We focus on meaning-preserving paraphrase as the attack vector, since it is both legally realistic and difficult to dismiss as evidence tampering.
  The results raise serious evidentiary concerns. Out of 846 valid paraphrase runs across 15 diverse prompts per method, every single initially-detected KGW and Unigram text lost its watermark after paraphrasing -- 100% conditional removal. SynthID fared only slightly better at 98.3%. Even before any attack, false-negative rates were already high: 70% for KGW, 83% for Unigram, 80% for SynthID. The SynthID configuration also flagged 5.4% of paraphrased human-written controls as AI-generated and showed an 18.6% paradox rate, with 80% of its own pristine watermarked output landing in the uncertainty deadband. None of the three methods satisfy more than two of five Daubert factors. We also find that the FRS point-based scoring system, despite working as designed, cannot fully capture forensic uselessness -- a limitation worth noting for future framework design.
  These configurations, as tested, do not meet the evidentiary bar that courts require.

</details>


### [121] [Gasp: A DeFi Application Specic Rollup as a Consolidation Layer for All Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16052)
*Stanislav Vozarik,Mateusz Nowakowski,Shoeb Siddiqui,Elliot Hill,Gleb Urvanov,Peter Kris*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Gasp是一个基于EigenLayer再质押ETH的、具备全链互联能力的应用特定L2乐观卷叠，旨在作为加密资产统一聚合层，实现无gas、原生跨链互换，并通过逃逸机制、Themis架构、流动性证明框架和时间奖励机制解决跨链通信中的安全性、MEV、资本效率和流动性承诺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前跨链通信面临安全性不足（传统桥接风险）、资产分级安全降级、MEV提取、流动性碎片化及资本效率低下等挑战，Gasp旨在构建一个无需桥接、保留L1原生安全的统一聚合层来解决这些问题。

Method: 1）采用应用特定L2乐观卷叠+EigenLayer再质押ETH确保计算正确性和最终性；2）全链互联架构实现原生跨链互换，无需传统桥接；3）逃逸机制保证提款安全性；4）Themis架构最小化MEV；5）流动性证明框架将质押与流动性提供结合，释放质押流动性；6）渐近奖励曲线的时间奖励机制激励长期流动性承诺。

Result: Gasp实现了无gas原生跨链资产互换，保留了L1级安全性，通过逃逸机制保障提款权，通过Themis降低MEV风险，通过流动性证明和时间奖励机制提升了资本效率和流动性深度。

Conclusion: Gasp通过综合架构创新为DeFi生态系统提供了跨链通信的全新解决方案，在安全性、资本效率和激励对齐方面取得了显著优化。

Abstract: Gasp is a decentralized exchange designed as an application-specific Layer 2 (L2) rollup with omnichain connectivity, leveraging EigenLayer's restaked ETH for computation correctness and finalization. With a goal of being a consolidation layer for all crypto assets, the Gasp platform employs optimistic rollup technology to facilitate gas-free, native cross-chain swaps without reliance on traditional bridges, ensuring tokens retain their original L1 grade security. By combining an app-chain architecture with escape hatch mechanisms, Gasp guarantees withdrawal, while MEV minimization through Themis architecture reduces value extraction risks. Gasp's proof-of-liquidity framework unlocks staked liquidity, enhancing capital efficiency and liquidity depth by integrating staking with liquidity provisioning. Additionally, the protocol introduces a time-based reward mechanism, incentivizing long-term liquidity commitment via an asymptotic reward curve. This paper examines the current challenges in cross-chain communication, delineates Gasp's architectural innovations and security guarantees, and examines novel approaches to optimizing DeFi ecosystems.

</details>


### [122] [DoSQ: A Cross-Layer Denial of Service Quality Attack by Exploiting Side Channels in 5G NR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16102)
*Mahmudul Hassan Ashik,Moinul Hossain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DoSQ的协议感知攻击，通过解码5G NR的DCI信息，利用跨层侧信道推断应用层Goodput状态，实现对受害者UE的精准干扰，最高可降低50%的Goodput，并提出了SSB跳频反制措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G系统面临智能干扰攻击的威胁，且传统观点认为空中接口广播的资源调度信息对用户设备无直接价值。作者发现DCI可被用作侧信道泄露应用层信息，从而引发跨层服务质量降级攻击。

Method: DoSQ攻击通过解码每时隙DCI，在1ms时隙内对受害者的PRB注入干扰；同时利用跨层分类器仅从DCI特征（不观察任何加密字节）估计受害者的Goodput状态和趋势。

Result: 在私有5G NR测试平台上针对YouTube Live实验，DoSQ以稀疏命中率将目标Goodput降低高达50%，且非目标UE几乎不受影响；分类器在攻击置信度前1%达到0.87的精确率，是基准率的4.21倍。

Conclusion: 首次实证测量了无线电到应用层的侧信道，该侧信道可被任何协议感知的对手利用；提出的SSB跳频反制措施可增加攻击者的重同步成本。

Abstract: The 3rd Generation Partnership Project (3GPP)'s Fifth Generation New Radio (5G NR) is critical to supporting mission-critical services. However, 5G systems are vulnerable to smart jamming attacks that can propagate to applications running on top of these networks (i.e., cross-layer). The 5G gNB broadcasts resource scheduling information for the legitimate UEs over the air interface, with a prevailing assumption that this surface alone reveals nothing useful about a user device. However, we show that using the Downlink Control Information (DCI) is sufficient to degrade Application layer service quality, i.e., Denial of Service Quality (DoSQ), by inferring the Application layer Goodput (i.e., via side-channel analysis). Therefore, we present DoSQ, a protocol-aware attack that decodes per-slot DCI to inject interference onto the victim UE's Physical Resource Blocks (PRBs) within the same 1 ms slot, while a cross-layer classifier estimates the victim's Goodput state and trend from DCI features alone, without observing a single encrypted byte. Evaluated on a private 5G NR testbed against YouTube Live, DoSQ drives the target's Goodput down by up to 50% at sparse hit-rates, while a co-located non-target UE remains largely unaffected. Moreover, the classifier achieves a precision of 0.87 at the top 1% of attack-now confidence, a 4.21 times lift over the base rate. Furthermore, we propose an SSB frequency-time-hopping countermeasure that increases the attacker's resynchronization cost. The result is the first empirical measurement of a radio-to-application side channel that any protocol-aware adversary can exploit.

</details>


### [123] [Evaluating Open-Weight LLMs for Generating Structured Threat Information for Autonomous Vehicle Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16175)
*Md Erfan,Ahmed Ryan,Md Kamal Hossain Chowdhury,Md Rayhanur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了CAV-STIXGen数据集，评估11个开源大模型（4B-120B参数）将CAV漏洞描述自动转换为STIX结构化威胁信息的能力。单模型在SDO、SRO和CWE映射上分别达到0.94、0.63和0.99的F1分数，但完整MITRE ATT&CK映射仍具挑战；多智能体设置下Gemma-4-31B和Codestral-22B分别取得0.91（SDO）和0.43（SRO）的F1分数。研究还分析了CWE与ATT&CK共现模式，展示了AI辅助漏洞到STIX翻译在自动化威胁情报和交通防御优先级排序中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CAV漏洞在CVE数据库中仅以纯文本记录，安全从业人员需要关于受影响资产、弱点类型和攻击行为的结构化信息来有效缓解风险，而现有方法缺乏将非结构化漏洞描述自动转换为结构化威胁信息（如STIX格式）的能力。

Method: 构建CAV-STIXGen数据集（包含CAV漏洞描述到SDO、SRO、CWE和MITRE ATT&CK技术映射），评估11个开源大语言模型（4B-120B参数）在不同提示策略和温度设置下的性能，并探索多智能体配置。

Result: 单模型在SDO映射上F1为0.94，SRO为0.63，CWE为0.99；完整MITRE ATT&CK映射困难。多智能体设置下Gemma-4-31B取得0.91（SDO），Codestral-22B取得0.43（SRO）。

Conclusion: AI辅助漏洞到STIX翻译可以自动化威胁情报生成并优先排序交通防御措施，但完整ATT&CK映射仍需改进。

Abstract: Connected and Autonomous Vehicles (CAVs) rely on interconnected software and hardware components, including sensors, Electronic Control Units, in-vehicle infotainment systems, and telematics units, where vulnerabilities can compromise assets, users, and vehicle operations. These vulnerabilities are commonly documented as plain text in the Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures (CVE) database; however, security practitioners require structured information about affected assets, types of weaknesses, and attack behaviors to effectively mitigate the risks from these vulnerabilities. To this end, we evaluate open-weight Large Language Models (LLMs) for generating Structured Threat Information Expression (STIX), a well-known structured format for representing threat information, for CAV-related CVEs. We construct a dataset called CAV-STIXGen that maps CAV vulnerability descriptions to STIX domain objects (SDO), STIX relationship objects (SRO), Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE), and MITRE ATT&CK techniques mappings. Using this dataset, we evaluated 11 open-weight LLMs (4B to 120B parameters) across various prompting strategies and temperatures. Single-model configurations achieve F1 scores of 0.94 for SDO, 0.63 for SRO, and 0.99 for CWE mapping, while complete MITRE ATT&CK mapping remains challenging. In a multi-agent setup, Gemma-4-31B and Codestral-22B achieve F1 scores of 0.91 for SDOs and 0.43 for SROs, respectively. Lastly, we analyze CWE and MITRE ATT&CK co-occurrences to identify recurring threat patterns in the CAV domain, demonstrating how AI-assisted vulnerability-to-STIX translation can automate threat intelligence and prioritize defense in transportation security.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Design-Based Supervised Learning with Noisy Human Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15455)
*Robert Chew,Matthew R. Williams*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出PA-DSL方法，利用裁定样本纠正噪声的人工标注，再基于修正后的审计信息去偏自动标注的分析结果，在合成和半合成实验中有效降低RMSE并保持名义覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纠正方法通常将人工审计标签视为正确，但实际中人工标注常含噪声，仅部分样本经专家裁定。需要一种能处理带噪声人工审计标签的纠偏方法。

Method: PA-DSL：利用已裁定样本纠正噪声人工标签，再用修正后的审计信息去偏基于自动标注的全集分析。方法适用于审计和裁定概率已知的场景。

Result: 在合成实验和Wikipedia Detox半合成实验中，相比仅使用裁定标签，PA-DSL保持名义覆盖率，RMSE降低10-17%。

Conclusion: 当噪声人工标签包含可恢复信号时，PA-DSL能有效提升自动标注分析的准确性和可靠性。

Abstract: Researchers increasingly use automated classifiers to label unstructured data for statistical analysis. Existing rectification methods can correct errors in these automated labels using a probability-sampled audit set, but they usually treat the audit labels as correct. In practice, human audit labels are often noisy, and only some audited items are reviewed by an expert or adjudicator. We propose Partially Adjudicated Design-Based Supervised Learning (PA-DSL), a method for this setting. It uses adjudicated cases to correct noisy human labels and then uses the corrected audit information to debias analyses based on the full set of automated labels. The estimator is valid for a broad class of downstream analyses when the audit and adjudication probabilities are known. In synthetic and Wikipedia Detox semi-synthetic experiments, PA-DSL maintains nominal coverage and reduces RMSE by 10-17% relative to using only adjudicated labels when noisy human labels contain recoverable signal.

</details>


### [125] [Retraining Seeks Stable Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15623)
*Moritz Hardt*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出稳定信号原则，证明当预测目标存在非零的模型无关成分（稳定信号）时，重复风险最小化（重新训练）会在适当正则化下几何收敛到该稳定信号的方向，即使模型对数据的影响很大。正则化在此起控制表演性的作用而非泛化，并拓展到仿射重训练算子、语言模型反馈循环等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表演性预测中，模型影响数据分布，重训练形成反馈循环。现有理论仅能处理模型影响较小的情况（收敛到不动点），但无法解释不动点为何自然存在，也无法刻画模型影响较大时的动态行为。本文旨在建立一套新理论框架来回答这两个问题。

Method: 提出稳定信号原则：假设预测目标包含至少一个非零的模型无关分量（如物品的内在质量）。在此基础上，对重复风险最小化过程进行正则化分析，证明其几何收敛到稳定信号方向。分析拓展到仿射重训练算子、异质时变效应、非线性响应以及语言模型中的数据反馈循环。

Result: 1) 存在非零稳定信号时，正则化重复风险最小化几何收敛到稳定信号方向；2) 即使模型影响任意大于稳定信号，收敛性依然成立；3) 正则化的新角色是控制表演性而非泛化；4) 理论可推广到仿射算子、时变效应、非线性响应；5) 为语言模型训练中模型生成数据的稳定性提供新解释。

Conclusion: 稳定信号原则统一了表演性预测中重训练过程的理论分析，揭示了正则化在表演性系统中的新功能，并为理解更广泛的学习系统（包括大语言模型的自训练）提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Predictive models deployed at scale influence future data, a phenomenon called performativity. And there is always one way to cope: Train the model on new data, deploy it again, and repeat. This process, called retraining or repeated risk minimization, creates a feedback loop between model and data that real-world learning systems can't avoid.
  Results on performative prediction shed light on this dynamic: If the model's influence on the data is small, retraining reaches a fixed point. What remains open is why fixed points should naturally exist, and what governs retraining when the model's influence is strong. In this work we develop a new perspective on retraining -- the stable signal principle -- that addresses these questions. We start from the assumption that the prediction target has at least some small model-independent component, a stable signal, such as the intrinsic quality of an item.
  We prove that when a nonzero stable signal exists, repeated risk minimization, suitably regularized, converges geometrically to the direction of this stable signal. This is true even if the model's influence on the target is arbitrarily large relative to the stable signal. Regularization emerges naturally as a force to control performativity, rather than to promote generalization, revealing a new facet of an old concept. We extend the analysis to a broad family of affine retraining operators under arbitrary model-induced feature changes, heterogeneous time-varying effects, and nonlinear responses. The stable signal perspective also applies to data feedback loops in language modeling, providing new explanations for the stability of language model training from model-generated data.

</details>


### [126] [Which Hyperparameters Matter? A Game-Theoretic Framework for Interpretable Hyperparameter Sensitivity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15884)
*Nyi Nyi Aung,Heepeom Shin,Abigail Lawlor,Adrian Stein*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于博弈论的超参数-目标交互分析框架，利用Shapley Effects进行全局敏感性分析，通过Pareto前沿集识别有效超参数配置，从而提供可解释的超参数与目标之间的交互洞察，并辅助早期模型评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超参数优化方法缺乏对超参数与不同优化目标之间交互关系的可解释性分析，研究者难以理解哪些超参数在特定目标下最为关键，无法有效指导后续优化方向。

Method: 采用博弈论框架，将超参数视为玩家、优化目标视为博弈结果；使用Shapley Effects进行全局敏感性分析以量化各超参数对目标的影响程度；利用Pareto前沿集识别最优超参数配置并支持早期模型评估。

Result: 揭示了不同超参数在多目标设置下对各目标的相对重要性，成功指导了搜索空间缩减和早期模型评估，并在三种不同神经网络架构和问题领域上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了目标感知的超参数交互可解释性分析，能够帮助实践者引导后续优化、减少搜索空间并进行早期模型评估，是一种分析工具而非新的优化算法。

Abstract: This work presents a game-theoretic framework for interpretable hyperparameter-objective interaction analysis rather than proposing a new optimization algorithm. In the proposed framework, Shapley Effects are employed for global sensitivity analysis, while Pareto front sets are utilized to identify effective hyperparameter configurations and support early-stage model evaluation. The resulting analysis reveals which players (hyperparameters) are most influential with respect to different objectives in a given game (application). Consequently, the proposed framework provides interpretable insights into objective-aware hyperparameter interactions, enabling practitioners to guide subsequent optimization, reduce the search space, and perform early-stage model evaluation. The effectiveness of the proposed framework is demonstrated using three distinct neural network architectures across different problem domains under multi-objective settings.

</details>


### [127] [Deep and Probabilistic Models for Gene Regulatory Network Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16053)
*Claudia Skok Gibbs*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了两个互补框架——PMF-GRN（基于概率图模型和变分推理的GRN推断）和GLM-Prior（利用预训练Nucleotide Transformer从序列预测TF-靶基因关系），前者实现原理性模型选择和不确定性感知的边估计，后者解决先验知识跨物种迁移瓶颈，共同推动双阶段GRN重建范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRN重建方法存在三个核心问题：（1）模型假设与推理过程耦合，依赖启发式模型选择；（2）评估受限于不完整参考网络和缺乏不确定性的点估计输出；（3）先验知识依赖特定实验条件，难以跨物种和低表征系统迁移。

Method: PMF-GRN将GRN推断形式化为概率图模型，通过变分推理进行优化；GLM-Prior微调预训练的Nucleotide Transformer，直接从核苷酸序列预测TF-靶基因相互作用，并在酵母、小鼠和人类三种物种上验证泛化能力。

Result: PMF-GRN提供了原理性模型选择和带不确定性的边估计；GLM-Prior实现了跨物种的TF-靶基因关系预测；两者结合形成双阶段框架：序列先验提供可迁移的初始支架，概率推断在不完全评估资源下精调调控估计并量化不确定性。

Conclusion: 序列驱动的先验与概率推断的互补结合，为GRN重建提供了一种更鲁棒、可迁移且具备不确定性量化的方法论路径，尤其适用于参考网络不完整或先验知识有限的生物系统。

Abstract: Gene regulatory networks (GRNs) link transcription factor (TF) proteins to their target genes, yet reconstructing these networks from genome-wide data remains challenging under practical and methodological constraints. Many methods couple modeling assumptions to a specific inference procedure and rely on heuristic model selection, while evaluation is constrained by incomplete reference networks and point-estimate outputs that lack uncertainty. GRN reconstruction also depends on prior knowledge to constrain TF-gene interactions, yet available priors are often assay-dependent and difficult to transfer across species and less-characterized systems. In this thesis, we develop two complementary frameworks that address these limitations. In the first, PMF-GRN casts GRN inference as a probabilistic graphical model optimized by variational inference, enabling principled model selection and uncertainty-aware edge estimates. In the second, GLM-Prior addresses the prior bottleneck by fine-tuning the pretrained Nucleotide Transformer to predict TF-target gene interactions directly from nucleotide sequence, while generalizing across yeast, mouse, and human settings. Together, PMF-GRN and GLM-Prior motivate a dual-stage view of GRN reconstruction in which sequence-derived priors provide a transferable starting scaffold and probabilistic inference refines regulatory estimates with quantified uncertainty under incomplete evaluation resources.

</details>


### [128] [Cluster-Aware Matching via Laplacian Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16178)
*Gabriel Samberg,YoonHaeng Hur,Yuehaw Khoo,Nir Sharon*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出基于拉普拉斯最优传输（LapOT）的聚类感知匹配方法，通过引入二次拉普拉斯正则化项鼓励最优传输耦合尊重点云的聚类结构，并设计了精细同步聚类（RSC）方法以生成跨点云的一致性分区，理论分析与实验验证了该方法在生成聚类感知匹配和有意义对齐方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多匹配应用中，点云并非无结构的点集，而是带有内在聚类结构的分布采样。此时单个点在连贯区域内可互换，建立鲁棒的"区域-区域"对齐比精确的"点-点"对应更有意义。现有方法未能充分利用这种聚类先验信息。

Method: 1) 构建点云的相似图，引入二次拉普拉斯项作为最优传输问题的正则化约束，鼓励传输耦合尊重双方的聚类结构；2) 提出精细同步聚类（RSC）方法，利用LapOT获得的聚类感知耦合生成跨点云的一致分区，克服独立聚类的不稳定性。

Result: 理论分析证明了LapOT确实能产生聚类感知的匹配；实验结果表明该方法相较于传统最优传输方法能产生更一致、更有意义的点云对齐。

Conclusion: LapOT通过拉普拉斯正则化有效将聚类先验融入最优传输框架，RSC实现了跨点云的聚类一致性，整体方法在聚类感知匹配任务中优于现有基准。

Abstract: In many applications of matching, the point clouds to be matched are not merely unstructured sets of points but rather samples from distributions with an intrinsic cluster structure. In such cases, as individual points are often interchangeable within a coherent region, finding a robust region-to-region alignment is more desirable than establishing a precise point-to-point correspondence. To this end, we propose a novel approach for cluster-aware matching based on Laplacian Optimal Transport (LapOT). The key idea is to regularize the optimal transport problem with quadratic Laplacian terms constructed from similarity graphs of the point clouds, which encourages the optimal coupling to respect the cluster structure of both point sets. We also introduce Refined Simultaneous Clustering (RSC), a method that leverages the cluster-aware coupling obtained from LapOT to produce consistent partitions across the point sets, which can overcome the limitations of independent clustering and yield more stable and interpretable results. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach through theoretical analysis and empirical experiments, showing that LapOT indeed produces cluster-aware matching that leads to more consistent and meaningful alignments between point clouds.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Prediction-Only Distillation in Linear and Logistic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15450)
*Hien Dang,Pratik Patil,Alessandro Rinaldo*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了无标签数据下的自蒸馏（Self-Distillation）问题，提出了一种fresh-X prediction-mixed方案：学生模型在新鲜无标签数据上通过教师伪标签训练，最终预测器为教师与学生预测的仿射组合。在岭回归和逻辑回归中证明该方法几乎总是优于教师模型，且最优混合权重可通过少量独立校准集一致估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中，原始标注训练数据常不可用，仅剩训练好的预测器和新无标签协变量。现有自蒸馏研究多假设可访问原训练数据，本文旨在研究无标签数据（prediction-only regime）下的自蒸馏有效性。

Method: 采用fresh-X prediction-mixed方案：学生模型在新鲜无标签协变量上通过教师伪标签训练，最终预测为教师与学生预测的仿射组合。对岭回归在比例渐近框架下推导确定性等价，对逻辑回归进行实验验证。

Result: 岭回归下，最优混合风险几乎对任意正则化水平组合均严格小于教师风险，包括分布外和各向同性协方差情况。最优混合权重需少量独立校准集估计。逻辑回归中预测混合可同时优于教师和纯蒸馏学生。

Conclusion: 在无原始训练数据、仅能访问教师预测器和新无标签数据的场景下，通过预测混合的自蒸馏方案几乎总能提升性能，且实际可行。

Abstract: Self-distillation (SD) is typically studied when the student is retrained on the teacher's original training inputs. In many practical deployments, however, the labeled training data are no longer available, and one has access only to the trained predictor and fresh unlabeled covariates. We study SD in this prediction-only regime through a fresh-X prediction-mixed scheme: a pure-distilled student is trained on fresh covariates pseudo-labeled by the teacher, and the final predictor is an affine combination of the teacher and student predictions. For ridge regression under proportional asymptotics, we derive deterministic equivalents for the optimally mixed prediction risk under general anisotropic covariance and deterministic signal. We show that this risk is strictly smaller than the teacher risk for almost every pair of teacher and student regularization levels, including when the fresh covariates are out-of-distribution and even when their covariance is isotropic. We further show that the optimal mixing weight cannot be identified from unlabeled data alone, but can be consistently estimated in a single post-training step using a small independent labeled calibration set, without additional model fitting. Finally, for binary logistic regression, we show that prediction mixing can outperform both the teacher and the pure-distilled classifier.

</details>


### [130] [Testing the rank of the spot covariance matrix of a multidimensional Itô semi-martingale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15945)
*Janine Steck*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对连续时间伊藤半鞅过程，提出了一种考虑适应性漂移影响的最大秩统计检验方法，通过重新中心化的协方差估计量替代传统二阶矩估计，在高频数据框架下推导了非渐近临界值，并证明了分离速率的一致性，模拟显示该方法比传统方法具有更高的检验功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于瞬时协方差矩阵秩的检验方法通常忽略适应性漂移项的影响，而本文证明这种忽略会导致显著偏差，因此需要开发一种能够明确考虑漂移效应的统计检验方法。

Method: 引入重新中心化的协方差估计量（re-centred covariance estimator）替代传统二阶矩估计量；在非渐近框架下推导临界值；针对原假设（秩≤r）与局部备择假设（第r+1个特征值超过某个衰减的信号检测率）进行检验；考虑漂移和瞬时协方差矩阵的Hölder正则性以及谱间隙的影响。

Result: 临界值可能受到漂移的显著影响；分离速率的一致性成立，该速率取决于漂移和瞬时协方差矩阵的Hölder正则性以及零假设下的谱间隙；模拟表明基于协方差的检验比传统二阶矩方法具有更高的检验功效。

Conclusion: 适应性漂移在瞬时协方差矩阵的秩检验中不可忽略，本文提出的重新中心化协方差估计量方法能够有效解决这一问题，并在非渐近框架下提供了可靠的推断工具。

Abstract: This work develops a statistical test for the maximal rank of the deterministic instantaneous (or spot) covariance matrix of a continuous-time $\mathbb{R}^d$-valued Itô semi-martingale $X(t)$ using high-frequency observations with a particular focus on the impact of an adapted drift. We explicitly account for the presence of an adapted drift process, which, as our results demonstrate, cannot be neglected, by introducing a re-centred covariance estimator instead of relying solely on a second moment estimator. Building on this estimator, we test the null hypothesis that the rank of the spot covariance matrix is at most $r<d$ for all $t$ against local alternatives in which the $(r+1)$th eigenvalue is greater than some vanishing signal detection rate. Critical values are derived in a non-asymptotic framework and can be significantly affected by a potential drift. However, the power analysis establishes asymptotic consistency for separation rates, which depend on the Hölder regularity of both the drift and the spot covariance matrix, as well as on a potential spectral gap $\underlineλ_r \geq 0$ under the null hypothesis. Simulation results indicate that the covariance-based test achieves higher power across a wider range of alternatives compared to classical second moment-based procedures.

</details>


### [131] [Local polynomial estimation of quantile density functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16016)
*Niclas Jacobsen,Natalie Neumeyer*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于局部多项式估计的非参数分位数密度函数（稀疏函数）及其导数的新估计方法。该估计量在边界处具有比经典分位数密度估计量更优的性质，并证明了渐近正态性，同时比较了偏差、渐近方差以及边界性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分位数密度估计方法在边界处表现不佳，存在较大的边界偏差。本文旨在通过引入局部多项式估计技术，提出一种在边界处具有更优性质的分位数密度函数估计方法，以克服经典估计量的缺陷。

Method: 采用局部多项式估计（Local Polynomial Estimation）方法对分位数密度函数（稀疏函数）及其导数进行非参数估计。该方法通过局部多项式拟合来估计分位数密度，从而改善边界处的估计性能。

Result: 证明了所提估计量的渐近正态性；在偏差、渐近方差以及边界性质方面，与经典分位数密度估计量进行了比较分析，结果表明新方法在边界处具有更优的性质。

Conclusion: 基于局部多项式估计的分位数密度函数估计方法在边界性质上优于传统方法，为分位数密度函数的非参数估计提供了一种更有效的替代方案。

Abstract: A new approach for nonparametric estimation of the quantile density function (sparsity function) and its derivatives is suggested which is based on local polynomial estimation. The estimator has more advantageous properties at the boundaries than classical quantile density estimators. Asymptotic normality is shown and the bias, asymptotic variance as well as boundary properties are compared with other estimators.

</details>


### [132] [Dimension-invariant uniform consistency of the empirical spatial distribution function and its associated spatial depth estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16092)
*Felix Gnettner,Hyemin Yeon,Piotr Kokoszka*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文证明了 $\mathbb R^d$ 中经验空间分布估计量及其对应的空间深度插件估计量具有一致 $L^1$-相合性，且相合速率仅依赖于样本量 $n$，而与维度 $d$ 或任何调参/正则化参数无关。该结果源于与 ChatGPT 5.4 Pro 的对话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索空间分布估计量和空间深度插件估计量的一致性是否能在不依赖维度 $d$ 的情况下实现，并验证其相合速率仅与样本量 $n$ 相关。

Method: 通过理论推导，证明经验空间分布估计量和空间深度插件估计量在 $\mathbb R^d$ 上的均匀 $L^1$-相合性。

Result: 得到了均匀 $L^1$-相合性，且相合速率仅依赖于 $n$，不依赖于 $d$ 或任何调参/正则化参数。

Conclusion: 该结果是罕见的，并源自与 ChatGPT 5.4 Pro 的对话。

Abstract: We provide a proof that the empirical spatial distribution estimator in $\mathbb R^d$ as well as the corresponding plug-in estimator of the spatial depth are uniformly $L^1$-consistent. The consistency rate only depends on the sample size $n$, not on the dimension $d$ or any tuning or regularization parameters. This is a rare property. The result of this note originates from a conversation with ChatGPT 5.4 Pro as part of some of our own earlier experiments on its mathematical reasoning capabilities.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [ADS-C: Antidistillation Sampling for Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15467)
*Khawaja Abaid Ullah,Mohammad Javad Khojasteh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ADS-C，一种针对分类任务的抗蒸馏防御方法。该方法通过在保持top-1预测不变的前提下，对输出概率分布施加输入相关的梯度扰动，使防御后教师模型准确率不变，但蒸馏学生的准确率大幅下降（CIFAR-100降17.4%，CIFAR-10降29.6%，Tiny-ImageNet降13.3%），同时发现直接迁移抗蒸馏采样存在"惰性窗口"和相变问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏使攻击者能通过查询预测接口复制专有分类器，现有抗蒸馏采样方法虽在LLM中有效但未在分类任务中验证，且直接迁移存在过置信导致的防御失效问题。需设计一种适用于分类任务、零效用损失的防御方法。

Method: 1. 分析抗蒸馏采样在分类任务中的行为，发现其受教师模型置信度边际分布控制，存在惰性窗口和相变阈值；2. 提出温度软化可闭合形式重新缩放相变点，但所有温度配置均在相同不利权衡曲线上；3. 提出ADS-C方法，在闭合形式的逐输入边际预算下构建扰动，严格保证每个served top-1预测不变，使防御后教师准确率与原教师完全相同。

Result: ADS-C在保持教师准确率不变的前提下：CIFAR-100学生准确率下降17.4%，CIFAR-10下降29.6%，Tiny-ImageNet下降13.3%；与之匹配的未修改防御需牺牲教师27.5、32.9、22.2个百分点的准确率。硬标签攻击者无收益，软输出训练的学生准确率比硬标签基线还低29.7个百分点。

Conclusion: ADS-C是首个实现零效用损失的分类抗蒸馏防御方法，不仅消除了蒸馏概率的激励，还将其逆转为惩罚，即蒸馏软输出反而比仅用硬标签训练更差。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation enables an adversary to replicate a proprietary classifier by querying its prediction interface and training a surrogate on the returned probability vectors. Antidistillation sampling, proposed for large language models, counters this threat with an input-dependent, gradient-directed perturbation of the served distribution; its transfer to classification has not been studied. Adapting the defense to classification, we show its behavior is governed by the distribution of the teacher's per-input confidence margins. Because well-trained classifiers are severely overconfident, the direct transfer exhibits an inert window: below a closed-form-predictable threshold, it affects neither attacker nor defender; beyond it, the defense undergoes a phase transition and degrades the teacher faster than the attacker's student. Temperature softening rescales the transition in closed form, and every temperature configuration lies on the same unfavorable trade-off curve. Our method, ADS-C, composes the perturbation under a closed-form, per-input margin budget that provably preserves every served top-1 prediction, so the defended teacher's accuracy equals the undefended teacher's identically. Under this guarantee the distilled student still loses 17.4 percentage points on CIFAR-100, 29.6 on CIFAR-10, and 13.3 on Tiny-ImageNet; matching this degradation with the unmodified defense costs 27.5, 32.9, and 22.2 points of teacher accuracy. Because served labels are unchanged, a hard-label attacker gains nothing, while the defended soft output trains a student up to 29.7 points below that floor: the incentive to distill served probabilities is not merely removed but reversed. To our knowledge, ADS-C is the first antidistillation defense for classification whose utility cost is exactly zero.

</details>


### [134] [Publicly-Verifiable Certificates for Statistical Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15528)
*Michael Ngo,Michael P. Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了非交互式学习证明的概念——公开可验证的统计有效性证书（pvCSVs），允许学习者在发布假设时附带证书，任何用户可根据自身分布高效验证假设的有效性。针对自适应统计查询（SQ）算法，构造了样本复杂度为O(log k)的pvCSVs，远低于最优学习算法的O~(√k)样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Goldwasser等人提出的交互式学习证明框架基础上，探索非交互式场景下的学习证明，使验证过程更高效、更易于公开部署。

Method: 定义公开可验证的统计有效性证书（pvCSVs）概念，针对自适应统计查询（SQ）算法设计构造方案，并分析SQ模型下证明系统的能力与局限。

Result: 对于进行k次自适应查询的SQ算法，pvCSVs的样本复杂度仅为O(log k)，而最优学习算法的样本复杂度为O~(√k)，实现了指数级的样本效率提升。

Conclusion: 非交互式学习证明在SQ模型中具有显著优势，但同时该模型也存在内在局限性。

Abstract: Following Goldwasser, Rothblum, Shafer, and Yehudayoff, who defined a framework for interactive proofs of learning [ITCS'21], we initiate the study of non-interactive proofs of learning. We define and study a new notion: Publicly-Verifiable Certificates of Statistical Validity (pvCSVs), which allow for public, distributionally-robust certification that the result of a learning algorithm is valid. In a pvCSV, a learner publishes a hypothesis $h$ and corresponding certificate $π$; then, any user, who holds a user-specific distribution, can read the pair $(h,π)$ and determine efficiently whether the hypothesis is valid according to the user-specific distribution.
  We construct pvCSVs in the context of Adaptive Statistical Query (SQ) Algorithms. To certify SQ algorithms that makes $k$ adaptive queries, we construct pvCSVs where the sample complexity scales with $O(\log k)$, whereas the sample complexity of the best learning algorithms scale with $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{k})$. More generally, we study proof systems for learning in the SQ model, demonstrating the model's strengths as well as its limitations.

</details>


### [135] [Structure of the Circular-Dyadic Convolution Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15293)
*Ben Fauber,Alireza Moradzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了用Hadamard变换替代FFT计算循环卷积时产生的代数误差，揭示了误差的结构化特性：两个输入/输出位置无误差、误差算子的零空间仅具有对数维数、期望误差由单一对齐标量控制，且误差能量渐近加倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
